Report to moderator Quick Reply Reply Quote Thank You Fight Club 24 Aug 2016 14:13 #2 omidfxt Topic Author --> Offline Associate Trader The Lower You Fall, The Higher You'll Fly Posts: 415 Thank you received: 1768 first of all i like to post the Price Reading Article here it's a precious gift from RTM and a wise guide for trading Price Reading In order to be able to trade the markets, we need to be able to understand why price is where it is, and where it will go to next. The best indicator for all this is Price itself. It holds all the clues you'll ever need to work the market out. We do our trading at levels called Supply and Demand Zones, and we watch Price Action there to give us signs as to the intentions of the big money. Here’s what we look for on the charts: General : HTF. Know where price is coming from and going to, and the PA past and present in all the TFs, from the Monthly down. Specific : At the zones you want to trade, look to: Past . Study the zone in all TFs, down to M1 ask yourself: Where were the decisions made? Clean S/D? Mark these lines. No clean S/D? – compressed zone Did price really shoot away form the zone, or did it cp away? Did the zone itself react at the right place? Look beyond the zone further into the past. See what it reacted to. Was there a better S/D nearby that price wants to visit? This explains many fakeouts. Did price originally react to the RS of a Flag Limit? It can fakeout to true SD of the FL. Present : Approach . How is price returning to the zone? Where’s the nearest flag in the TF you want to trade? This is your tg1 in this TF. Flags in the LTFs? What does PA tell you? Has price tested the last flag on approach? (good sign) Has price compressed into the zone in this TF or LTFs? (good sign) Is there big news on the way? Has there just been big news? Reaction . In LTF, does price react violently to the first decision point? Does it quickly engulf the nearest S/D? (good sign) Does price simply CP away? Maybe it wants to go to the next decision point If the first decision point breaks, watch the signs on approach to the next, and, of course, reaction. Report to moderator Quick Reply Reply Quote Thank You Fight Club 10 Sep 2016 15:01 #22 corsica --> Offline Associate Trader Posts: 291 Thank you received: 507 Hi Omidfxt Thank you for posting these charts. I think you are struggling because the difference Flag- Flag limit is not clear. I marked some on your chart. What is crucial to understand is that a FL is continuation pattern, a DBD or a RBR which engulfs a SR. the more important the SR the more valuable your FL is. The Homework thread about FL is really good. particularly the work done by Aaron. It is also very easy after the fact so I spotted a MPL too, it is at QM level ( where people put their stops instead of putting their entry ) You mark these limits perfectly, you just need to clarify a bit SR- FL and you're ready to rock ! Cheers Attachments: Check this box to be notified of replies to this topic. Note: BBcode and smileys are still usable. I think I'm exaggerating, but as my scripts occasionally do funny things because of random gaps in certain TFs, I'm now used to update them very frequently. Sorry for the intromission. they needs lots of time to be answered(and i'm not really enough experienced to answer all of them ) and lots of chart. so i just tried to answer them from my point of view and know that they are not enough! and beg my pardon if i miss understand some of your question while answering about the first target, generally the answer is yes but it really depends, for example if price engulfed the last FL on approach before reaching our entry then the first target can be the next one and you can get more pips! and about the origin for the target, it depend again! if origin be far away it will be better to chose a closer FL for the first target and for the final target, yes origin can be a good target depends on the multi time frame analysis and the engulfed that occurred in the chart and yes MPLs can be the origin of the move too Second engulfs can be really great when they happen into free space! but depends on the situation, sometimes after the second engulfed you don't have enough space to enter and it's possible that there will not be a good retrace to enter with, and we have to be careful that second engulfed haven't reached it's target! about a FTR that got faked out, if you mean the QM lvl, and MPLs, we have to always look for them in the FL! there are a lot of QM lvl shapes in the chart but the best ones are the ones that occurred in a FL! about CPAZ, e.g when the last Demand in the CP got engulfed we can expect that price goes through the CP zone and we have to make sure that priced cleared all the pockets of Demands while going up! but most of the time the CPs aren't perfect, if it's in a proper zone, it we be really nice to trade it and the cp in your chart, if we consider the engulf of that FL that you spotted, that will be a good sign for entering with the retrace by the way your charts are really artistic Check this box to be notified of replies to this topic. Report to moderator Quick Reply Reply Quote Thank You Fight Club 15 Nov 2016 12:35 #159 Edge --> Offline Associate Trader Posts: 481 Thank you received: 1306 I love how you analyse winners and losers. Great stuff.... That's the thing about the FLs. We seem to only know the true FL as it occurs. It can be hidden right down in the tick chart and if we don't have the data what appears as the FL in a slightly HTF may actually be a reaction to the tiny FL where the decision was to fail and continue. I guess that's why we have to keep drilling down, unless we have a larger retrace after the barrier than failure and continuation. In that case the FL can be seen on the HTF. Would that be correct? The following user(s) said Thank You: omidfxt , cuimingzhao Report to moderator Quick Reply Reply Quote Thank You Fight Club 16 Nov 2016 05:01 #170 omidfxt Topic Author --> Offline Associate Trader The Lower You Fall, The Higher You'll Fly Posts: 415 Thank you received: 1768 OK Guys It is about 8 month that i am living in RTM and enjoyed every second of it. RTM really feel like home for me! from the first moment i start RTM, i forgot every bullshit that i studied before, candle sticks, trendlines, channels... and stop trading live and put all my time into learning RTM concepts I Start with the Homeworks, articles and videos. I studied them Step by Step and had plan for each part of my journey yet Also if you study the RTM from the first pages, start here, everything has been said there and a complete plan for your trading journey has been told there After doing homeworks for about 4-5 months! i went through the journals and at the same time started trading on a demo account for two months that i post it's result in the previous pages in my journal, that was a turning point in my trading style and understanding! I decided not to trade live until i become more comfortable with the market and become profitable at least for three months as IF said I think it worth to reading it again:( Resource Link ) A Note before you begin To all new traders: This is a business, a very hard business.Every day we stare into the abyss, and the abyss stares right back into us.To make our living, we must daily face the demons Fear and Greed, and win. Most of us, upon beginning our journey in trading, blew account after account , trying out any system which was advertised well enough. We found out that the market could chew us up and spit us out without giving us a thought. We certainly paid our dues to that which would provide for us later. We imagined that we'd get rich quick, and that just didn't happen so easily. What we had to do was put away all those silly indicators that promised to give us an edge, and just look at what the charts were showing us.It took thousands of hours of hard work and dedication to become successful traders. Eventually, we found that price has ways of telling you what it's going to do next. We began to notice many different patterns and price structures which indicate that price should move to here or there. We were no longer looking for an "edge". We just saw the truth of the market - what it wanted to do next. . . . Begin trading in Demo I know you're itching to make your millions right away. You will not. You will not hit the ground running. You will hit the ground rolling and stumbling and not knowing where your feet are. Make all your initial mistakes for free. Become regularly profitable for three months before you trade with real money. Every loser can have a winning streak and think he's cracked the market. You will have winning streaks and losing streaks along the way. This is part of the learning process. Imagine that you need a trading license to trade live, and that you must be consistently profitable for at least three months to earn that license.Don't create any shortcuts or loopholes. Earn that license fairly and honestly, or it'll cost you, I promise. When you've earned your trading license, celebrate. Have a party if you like. Award yourself the license, either physically or mentally. You'll need that memory to cling to in the dark times ahead. When you're ready to trade live, remember this: Trading live is a world away from trading demo. The emotions you will feel will be far removed from those you experienced in demo. Sure, you are ready to read the market, but now is the time to master your emotions. This may take time. Fear and greed can be as destructive as those silly systems you left behind. Successful traders don't just take money magically from the markets. They take it from PEOPLE. They take it from the 95% of people who try to trade and fail. Don't let them see YOUR money in the market until you're one of them. So as you read above IF advise us not to start trading Live till we become profitable at least for 3 months i read this at the start of my journey and promised to my self to put my believe in IF's words and run the exact plan After my previous demo account RTM gave a promotion on my rate in the site that was an amazing time for me and i was really excited i start another Demo account and tried to do my best. but as i was in a new situation(first days of promotion) i really didn't have enough concentrate on this account so i didn't do very well on it especially at the first few weeks i want to show you that one month result: after that here is another account that i worked with it only for two weeks yet: so we can add this statement to the previous one As most of the time i try to trade the edges(As IF emphasis on it) so always my stops are small and rewards are huge. this is what make RTM unique in compare to other methods! I don't know if the result are good or not but This is the reality and what i really am! i always like to be myself and by seeing what is the reality about my functions i can work on my weakness and improve faster So after being profitable in demo accounts for about three months and becoming more comfortable with the market recently i think it's the time to start a Live account as another step in my learning process Now it's time for me to learn how to control the most important things in trading: Emotions ! i know that i'm not still perfect in trading and still needs a lot to learn but i think starting a live account in this stage will help me a lot to become a better traders I had plan for each step and have one for this one too My next step in my trading journey is to become more accurate in finding targets and take more from the market Here is my new Live account Chart: I always like it to have a plan and try to to my best and i'm gonna do it again and again With best wishes for you guys omid Attachments: Check this box to be notified of replies to this topic. The following user(s) said Thank You: pardis , omidfxt , cuimingzhao Report to moderator Quick Reply Reply Quote Thank You Fight Club 17 Nov 2016 21:39 #181 omidfxt Topic Author --> Offline Associate Trader The Lower You Fall, The Higher You'll Fly Posts: 415 Thank you received: 1768 Hey Guys here is a brief explanation on how the market works and how the main player try to win the game Nothing is new here, all had been said thorough RTM. Just want to share it with my friends as a remind: Before you want to join the game you HAVE TO know who are your enemies and how they play the game! Why you have to know them? Just a simple logic: Because they are the main players in the game and the ones who are changing the market conditions Certainly they are in the market to make money for their investors and absolutely themselves! So you can’t be a good player if you don’t know the games rules and what there are looking for! As you know nobody isn’t in the market just for the sake of God! All the players in this game are looking for MONEY! The same reason you are here! Aren’t you? : ) Here we can specify Forex players: (image resource;forextraderportal.com) All the players above want to increase their capitals and made more money So as a simple logic there isn’t such a condition for everyone to profit from the market As In the real world if you want to sell something you have to find someone to buy it from you and if there isn’t any buyers so your property doesn’t have any value! And if there are lots of buyers, there will be a completion between them to owe your property so the value of your property will raise and you can sell it in a higher price! It is the same in the market. It is all about supply and demand! Ok. Let’s get back to the main players in the market They are all in a competition and want to make money from the market But between them there some BIG GUYS that can make more money and no ones is going to stop them! Why? Simple because they have the money and certainly the power! If they want they can smash everyone on their way! And they do! Because they want it all! So how can they do it? Again simple! They cheat on other ones! And if they want the money they have to do it!! Because for example if they want to sell loads of something they have to have loads of buyers for their sell orders. That simple! Ok. Now we know who are they and what they want. Great! Now let’s see how it’s possible for them to achieve their goals? First of all let’s talk about one of the participators in the market: Retail Traders! Most of the time retail traders are known as individual traders that are ordinary people that are trying to make money from the market(and in 95% of the time they can’t!) But I think we can call someone as a retail trader that is not a BIG GUYS! An individual trader, an institute traders or even a bank trader that is not in the BIG GUYS band can be a retailer!! Now we are familiar with the retailers that are an easy competitor for BIG GUYS! What are the BGs(Big Guys)’s plan for them? Simple again! They interest everyone into trading and teach them how to do it wrong! Brokers are some of their best friends! Brokers make the deal and profit from the spreads and commissions! Easy money for them ! So if they have enough customers they can make more money. Ok. Simple work! Just try to advertise for trading and show it as simple as possible! They try to provide sexy platforms and apps for trading and lots of artistic indicator! You can interest everybody into trading just by a simple indicator and a beautiful trend line or channel! They translate their false trading strategies that are prepared very smart into many language and give them to you just for free! Yeah they really love retailers in the deepest part of their hearts! They show you: oh look at this beautiful channel. Every time price hit one side, it will react there and will go to the other side. Also look at that beautiful MACD, RSI, … indicator that is telling you when to trade and when to exit! So amazing. Who is enough silly not to start trading after seeing this! Absolutely no one as most of us did! : ) So let’s start with the free education and after a short period of time we can start trading and open a live account to make the millions that we were dreaming all the times! Ok a good and sexy plan! But most of the retailers don’t know who are behind the sense that are playing the games! Smart and experienced traders teams with millions in their asset on their trading desk! They are there for retail trader’s money and will make them confused each time! Retailers: Damn! my stop just got hit by a few pips and then price went in my direction! Ok. That because the lake of my understanding. I will study more and will do it again. There is a huge amount of money right there, I can’t give up on it simply! That is the story that will be repeat again and again and HAS TO be repeat to full the pocket of BGs and their friends! Just simple as always! ........................................................................................................................................................................................... So as an advantage of knowing the main players in the market and their goals: We as Smart retailers : ) have to be aware of all the tricks that are played in the market As RTM told us many times, we have to try to get into the mind of the BGs and put their hats on our heads and look at the chart as ones does! We have to think in terms of order flow. Where BGs can fill their orders? Where they can trap others to provide opposite positions for them to fill their orders? RTM showed us how to see the manipulation of hope and fear and think about the retailer’s mindset. How to see the buildings of positions and the real movement of price and the reasons behind them If we study all the RTM concepts they are all saying that what are BGs doing in the market and where they are looking for trapping other retailers RTM is telling us always looking for signs of what the BGs are doing at the significant areas and emphasize on it that if we don’t see what they are doing, there will be no trade for us! If you seek through RTM and all the Concepts and the trades IF and masters took you can see it clearly what is going on the market and you will understand how to READ THE MARKET properly This was just a brief and simple explanation of what I have learned so far from this community and want to share it with my friends You can read more through RTM and some very limited sources in the Internet Know your enemies and their intentions and don't let them fool you : ) Best Regards omid Attachments: Check this box to be notified of replies to this topic. Hi, Here is what I see: ORFL -Down to the left on a lower time frame: We have a reaction (supply) and a reaction to the supply (Origin). Origin is tested and bounces price (ORFL). Then, ORFL Down is itself engulfed and a new FL Box (red) is formed. When the red FL Box it engulfed another FL Box is formed and we have a swap zone. Like before, we have a reaction to a FL (the FL is neon green). There is also an engulf of a very small ORFL down, which occurs before the larger reaction to the green FL. Just like before, price engulfs this zone and another ORFL is formed. Test 1 to create supply: After supply is formed by testing the ORFL Down, an opportunity to FO to MPL (deeper in to the ORFL) exists and price reacts accordingly. I think this is why Omid went short at that level. Attachments: Check this box to be notified of replies to this topic. Note: BBcode and smileys are still usable. The following user(s) said Thank You: pardis , cuimingzhao "Cobalah terus sampai kegagalanmu habis tak bersisa" mangdsd Report to moderator Quick Reply Reply Quote Thank You Fight Club 01 Jan 2017 16:04 #224 hoeschec --> Offline Associate Trader Posts: 1795 Thank you received: 2480 samjardavid wrote: hi hoeschec ^_^ what the different between ORFL and FL? is ORFL more important than FL Thank you in advance David Hi Samjardavid- A FL is any FTR that holds price up or down whereas a ORFL is an origin up/down and a FL. ORFL is often the target of longs/shorts, so they hold special importance. Salivan discusses the concept of magnetic attraction vs. repulsion and this is related specifically to ORFL's. QUICK REPLY REPLY QUOTE THANK YOU https://www.readthemarket.com/index.php/en/forum/journals/2567-fight-club 2/45 7/27/2018 Fight Club - RTM Community Fight Club EDUCATION FORUM MORE LOGOUT #2 HOME omidfxt Offline Associate Trader The Lower You Fall, The Higher You'll Fly Posts: 415 Thank you received: 1791 first of all i like to post the Price Reading Article here it's a precious gift from RTM and a wise guide for trading Price Reading In order to be able to trade the markets, we need to be able to understand why price is where it is, and where it will go to next. The best indicator for all this is Price itself. It holds all the clues you'll ever need to work the market out. We do our trading at levels called Supply and Demand Zones, and we watch Price Action there to give us signs as to the intentions of the big money. Here's what we look for on the charts: General: HTF. Know where price is coming from and going to, and the PA past and present in all the TFs, from the Monthly down. Specific: At the zones you want to trade, look to: Past. Study the zone in all TFs, down to M1 ask yourself: Where were the decisions made? Clean S/D? Mark these lines. No clean S/D? - compressed zone Did price really shoot away form the zone, or did it cp away? https://www.readthemarket.com/index.php/en/forum/journals/2567-fight-club 3/45 7/27/2018 Fight Club - RTM Community Did the zone itself react at the right place? Look beyond the zone further into the past. HOME EDUCATION FORUM MORE LOGOUT See what it reacted to. Was there a better S/D nearby that price wants to visit? This explains many fakeouts. Did price originally react to the RS of a Flag Limit? It can fakeout to true SD of the FL. Present: Approach. How is price returning to the zone? Where's the nearest flag in the TF you want to trade? This is your tg1 in this TF. Flags in the LTFs? What does PA tell you? Has price tested the last flag on approach? (good sign) Has price compressed into the zone in this TF or LTFs? (good sign) Is there big news on the way? Has there just been big news? Reaction. In LTF, does price react violently to the first decision point? Does it quickly engulf the nearest S/D? (good sign) Does price simply CP away? Maybe it wants to go to the next decision point If the first decision point breaks, watch the signs on approach to the next, and, of course, reaction. and beg my pardon if i miss understand some of your question while answering about the first target, generally the answer is yes but it really depends, for example if price engulfed the last FL on approach before reaching our entry then the first target can be the next one and you can get more pips! and about the origin for the target, it depend again! if origin be far away it will be better to chose a closer FL for the first target and for the final target, yes origin can be a good target depends on the multi time frame analysis and the engulfed that occurred in the chart and yes MPLs can be the origin of the move too Second engulfs can be really great when they happen into free space! but depends on the situation, sometimes after the second engulfed you don't have enough space to enter and it's possible that there will not be a good retrace to enter with, and we have to be careful that second engulfed haven't reached it's target! about a FTR that got faked out, if you mean the QM lvl, and MPLs, we have to always look for them in the FL! there are a lot of QM lvl shapes in the chart but the best ones are the ones that occurred in a FL! about CPAZ, e.g when the last Demand in the CP got engulfed we can expect that price goes through the CP zone and we have to make sure that priced cleared all the pockets https://www.readthemarket.com/index.php/en/forum/journals/2567-fight-club?start=100 13/44 7/27/2018 Fight Club - Page 5 - RTM Community of Demands while going up! Most of us, upon beginning our journey in trading, blew account after account , trying out any system which was advertised well enough. We found out that the market could chew us up and spit us out without giving us a thought. We certainly paid our dues to that which would provide for us later. We imagined that we'd get rich quick, and that just didn't happen so easily. What we had to do was put away all those silly indicators that promised to give us an edge, and just look at what the charts were showing us.It took thousands of hours of hard work and dedication to become successful traders. Eventually, we found that price has ways of telling you what it's going to do next. We began to notice many different patterns and price structures which indicate that price should move to here or there. We were no longer looking for an "edge". We just saw the truth of the market - what it wanted to do next. https://www.readthemarket.com/index.php/en/forum/journals/2567-fight-club?start=150 31/43 7/27/2018 Fight Club - Page 7 - RTM Community . HOME EDUCATION FORUM MORE LOGOUT . . Begin trading in Demo I know you're itching to make your millions right away. You will not. You will not hit the ground running. You will hit the ground rolling and stumbling and not knowing where your feet are. Make all your initial mistakes for free. Become regularly profitable for three months before you trade with real money. Every loser can have a winning streak and think he's cracked the market. You will have winning streaks and losing streaks along the way. This is part of the learning process. Imagine that you need a trading license to trade live, and that you must be consistently profitable for at least three months to earn that license.Don't create any shortcuts or loopholes. Earn that license fairly and honestly, or it'll cost you, I promise. When you've earned your trading license, celebrate. Have a party if you like. Award yourself the license, either physically or mentally. You'll need that memory to cling to in the dark times ahead. When you're ready to trade live, remember this: Trading live is a world away from trading demo. The emotions you will feel will be far removed from those you experienced in demo. Sure, you are ready to read the market, but now is the time to master your emotions. This may take time. Fear and greed can be as destructive as those silly systems you left behind. Successful traders don't just take money magically from the markets. They take it from PEOPLE. They take it from the 95% of people who try to trade and fail. Don't let them see YOUR money in the market until you're one of them. Hi, 40/50 https://www.readthemarket.com/index.php/en/forum/journals/2567-fight-club?start=200 7/27/2018 Fight Club - Page 9 - RTM Community HOME EDUCATION FORUM MORE LOGOUT Here is what I see: ORFL -Down to the left on a lower time frame: We have a reaction (supply) and a reaction to the supply (Origin). Origin is tested and bounces price (ORFL). Then, ORFL Down is itself engulfed and a new FL Box (red) is formed. https://www.readthemarket.com/index.php/en/forum/journals/2567-fight-club?start=200 41/50 7/27/2018 Fight Club - Page 9 - RTM Community HOME EDUCATION FORUM MORE LOGOUT When the red FL Box it engulfed another FL Box is formed and we have a swap zone. Like before, we have a reaction to a FL (the FL is neon green). There is also an engulf of a very small ORFL down, which occurs before the larger reaction to the green FL. Just like before, price engulfs this zone and another ORFL is formed. Test 1 to create supply: https://www.readthemarket.com/index.php/en/forum/journals/2567-fight-club?start=200 42/50 7/27/2018 Fight Club - Page 9 - RTM Community HOME EDUCATION FORUM MORE LOGOUT After supply is formed by testing the ORFL Down, an opportunity to FO to MPL (deeper in to the ORFL) exists and price reacts accordingly. I think this is why Omid went short at that level. Posts: 51 Thank you received: 61 hi hoeschec ^_^ what the different between ORFL and FL? is ORFL more important than FL Thank you in advance David The following user(s) said Thank You: pardis, cuimingzhao "Cobalah terus sampai kegagalanmu habis tak bersisa" mangdsd QUICK REPLY REPLY QUOTE THANK YOU Fight Club #224 https://www.readthemarket.com/index.php/en/forum/journals/2567-fight-club?start=200 44/50 7/27/2018 Fight Club - Page 9 - RTM Community HOME EDUCATION FORUM MORE LOGOUT hoeschec Offline Associate Trader Posts: 1795 Thank you received: 2487 samjardavid wrote: hi hoeschec ^_^ what the different between ORFL and FL? is ORFL more important than FL Thank you in advance David Hi Samjardavid- A FL is any FTR that holds price up or down whereas a ORFL is an origin up/down and a FL. ORFL is often the target of longs/shorts, so they hold special importance. Salivan discusses the concept of magnetic attraction vs. repulsion and this is related specifically to ORFL's. Report to moderator Quick Reply Reply Quote Thank You Fight Club 24 Aug 2016 14:13 #2 omidfxt Topic Author --> Offline Associate Trader The Lower You Fall, The Higher You'll Fly Posts: 415 Thank you received: 1768 first of all i like to post the Price Reading Article here it's a precious gift from RTM and a wise guide for trading Price Reading In order to be able to trade the markets, we need to be able to understand why price is where it is, and where it will go to next. The best indicator for all this is Price itself. It holds all the clues you'll ever need to work the market out. We do our trading at levels called Supply and Demand Zones, and we watch Price Action there to give us signs as to the intentions of the big money. Here’s what we look for on the charts: General : HTF. Know where price is coming from and going to, and the PA past and present in all the TFs, from the Monthly down. Specific : At the zones you want to trade, look to: Past . Study the zone in all TFs, down to M1 ask yourself: Where were the decisions made? Clean S/D? Mark these lines. No clean S/D? – compressed zone Did price really shoot away form the zone, or did it cp away? Did the zone itself react at the right place? Look beyond the zone further into the past. See what it reacted to. Was there a better S/D nearby that price wants to visit? This explains many fakeouts. Did price originally react to the RS of a Flag Limit? It can fakeout to true SD of the FL. Present : Approach . How is price returning to the zone? Where’s the nearest flag in the TF you want to trade? This is your tg1 in this TF. Flags in the LTFs? What does PA tell you? Has price tested the last flag on approach? (good sign) Has price compressed into the zone in this TF or LTFs? (good sign) Is there big news on the way? Has there just been big news? Reaction . In LTF, does price react violently to the first decision point? Does it quickly engulf the nearest S/D? (good sign) Does price simply CP away? Maybe it wants to go to the next decision point If the first decision point breaks, watch the signs on approach to the next, and, of course, reaction. (as you know HTF target can be another FL. after engulfing a FL price will go to the next FL in the direction of the engulf(need multi time frame analysis and is not as easy as a piece of cake for me yet )) you explanations on the chart was really good, nothing especial to add, just a few note on one of your chart Regards omidFxt Attachments: Check this box to be notified of replies to this topic. The following user(s) said Thank You: omidfxt , salivan , cuimingzhao Report to moderator Quick Reply Reply Quote Thank You Fight Club 19 Oct 2016 03:19 #107 omidfxt Topic Author --> Offline Associate Trader The Lower You Fall, The Higher You'll Fly Posts: 415 Thank you received: 1768 hey micka1 all the question you are asking are really important and deep! they needs lots of time to be answered(and i'm not really enough experienced to answer all of them ) and lots of chart. so i just tried to answer them from my point of view and know that they are not enough! and beg my pardon if i miss understand some of your question while answering about the first target, generally the answer is yes but it really depends, for example if price engulfed the last FL on approach before reaching our entry then the first target can be the next one and you can get more pips! and about the origin for the target, it depend again! if origin be far away it will be better to chose a closer FL for the first target and for the final target, yes origin can be a good target depends on the multi time frame analysis and the engulfed that occurred in the chart and yes MPLs can be the origin of the move too Second engulfs can be really great when they happen into free space! but depends on the situation, sometimes after the second engulfed you don't have enough space to enter and it's possible that there will not be a good retrace to enter with, and we have to be careful that second engulfed haven't reached it's target! about a FTR that got faked out, if you mean the QM lvl, and MPLs, we have to always look for them in the FL! there are a lot of QM lvl shapes in the chart but the best ones are the ones that occurred in a FL! about CPAZ, e.g when the last Demand in the CP got engulfed we can expect that price goes through the CP zone and we have to make sure that priced cleared all the pockets of Demands while going up! but most of the time the CPs aren't perfect, if it's in a proper zone, it we be really nice to trade it and the cp in your chart, if we consider the engulf of that FL that you spotted, that will be a good sign for entering with the retrace by the way your charts are really artistic Check this box to be notified of replies to this topic. Great stuff.... That's the thing about the FLs. We seem to only know the true FL as it occurs. I guess that's why we have to keep drilling down, unless we have a larger retrace after the barrier than failure and continuation. In that case the FL can be seen on the HTF. Would that be correct? RTM really feel like home for me! from the first moment i start RTM, i forgot every bullshit that i studied before, candle sticks, trendlines, channels... and stop trading live and put all my time into learning RTM concepts I Start with the Homeworks, articles and videos. I studied them Step by Step and had plan for each part of my journey yet Also if you study the RTM from the first pages, start here, everything has been said there and a complete plan for your trading journey has been told there After doing homeworks for about 4-5 months! i went through the journals and at the same time started trading on a demo account for two months that i post it's result in the previous pages in my journal, that was a turning point in my trading style and understanding! I decided not to trade live until i become more comfortable with the market and become profitable at least for three months as IF said I think it worth to reading it again:( Resource Link ) A Note before you begin To all new traders: This is a business, a very hard business.Every day we stare into the abyss, and the abyss stares right back into us.To make our living, we must daily face the demons Fear and Greed, and win. Most of us, upon beginning our journey in trading, blew account after account , trying out any system which was advertised well enough. We found out that the market could chew us up and spit us out without giving us a thought. We certainly paid our dues to that which would provide for us later. We imagined that we'd get rich quick, and that just didn't happen so easily. What we had to do was put away all those silly indicators that promised to give us an edge, and just look at what the charts were showing us.It took thousands of hours of hard work and dedication to become successful traders. Eventually, we found that price has ways of telling you what it's going to do next. We began to notice many different patterns and price structures which indicate that price should move to here or there. We were no longer looking for an "edge". We just saw the truth of the market - what it wanted to do next. . . . Begin trading in Demo I know you're itching to make your millions right away. You will not. You will not hit the ground running. You will hit the ground rolling and stumbling and not knowing where your feet are. Make all your initial mistakes for free. Become regularly profitable for three months before you trade with real money. Every loser can have a winning streak and think he's cracked the market. You will have winning streaks and losing streaks along the way. This is part of the learning process. Imagine that you need a trading license to trade live, and that you must be consistently profitable for at least three months to earn that license.Don't create any shortcuts or loopholes. Earn that license fairly and honestly, or it'll cost you, I promise. When you've earned your trading license, celebrate. Have a party if you like. Award yourself the license, either physically or mentally. You'll need that memory to cling to in the dark times ahead. When you're ready to trade live, remember this: Trading live is a world away from trading demo. The emotions you will feel will be far removed from those you experienced in demo. Sure, you are ready to read the market, but now is the time to master your emotions. This may take time. Fear and greed can be as destructive as those silly systems you left behind. Successful traders don't just take money magically from the markets. They take it from PEOPLE. They take it from the 95% of people who try to trade and fail. Don't let them see YOUR money in the market until you're one of them. So as you read above IF advise us not to start trading Live till we become profitable at least for 3 months i read this at the start of my journey and promised to my self to put my believe in IF's words and run the exact plan After my previous demo account RTM gave a promotion on my rate in the site that was an amazing time for me and i was really excited i start another Demo account and tried to do my best. but as i was in a new situation(first days of promotion) i really didn't have enough concentrate on this account so i didn't do very well on it especially at the first few weeks i want to show you that one month result: after that here is another account that i worked with it only for two weeks yet: so we can add this statement to the previous one As most of the time i try to trade the edges(As IF emphasis on it) so always my stops are small and rewards are huge. this is what make RTM unique in compare to other methods! The following user(s) said Thank You: pardis , omidfxt , cuimingzhao Report to moderator Quick Reply Reply Quote Thank You Fight Club 17 Nov 2016 21:39 #181 omidfxt Topic Author --> Offline Associate Trader The Lower You Fall, The Higher You'll Fly Posts: 415 Thank you received: 1768 Hey Guys here is a brief explanation on how the market works and how the main player try to win the game Nothing is new here, all had been said thorough RTM. Just want to share it with my friends as a remind: Before you want to join the game you HAVE TO know who are your enemies and how they play the game! Why you have to know them? Just a simple logic: Because they are the main players in the game and the ones who are changing the market conditions Certainly they are in the market to make money for their investors and absolutely themselves! So you can’t be a good player if you don’t know the games rules and what there are looking for! As you know nobody isn’t in the market just for the sake of God! All the players in this game are looking for MONEY! The same reason you are here! Aren’t you? : ) Here we can specify Forex players: (image resource;forextraderportal.com) All the players above want to increase their capitals and made more money So as a simple logic there isn’t such a condition for everyone to profit from the market As In the real world if you want to sell something you have to find someone to buy it from you and if there isn’t any buyers so your property doesn’t have any value! And if there are lots of buyers, there will be a completion between them to owe your property so the value of your property will raise and you can sell it in a higher price! It is the same in the market. It is all about supply and demand! Ok. Let’s get back to the main players in the market They are all in a competition and want to make money from the market But between them there some BIG GUYS that can make more money and no ones is going to stop them! Why? Simple because they have the money and certainly the power! If they want they can smash everyone on their way! And they do! Because they want it all! So how can they do it? Again simple! They cheat on other ones! And if they want the money they have to do it!! Because for example if they want to sell loads of something they have to have loads of buyers for their sell orders. That simple! Ok. Now we know who are they and what they want. Great! Now let’s see how it’s possible for them to achieve their goals? First of all let’s talk about one of the participators in the market: Retail Traders! Most of the time retail traders are known as individual traders that are ordinary people that are trying to make money from the market(and in 95% of the time they can’t!) But I think we can call someone as a retail trader that is not a BIG GUYS! An individual trader, an institute traders or even a bank trader that is not in the BIG GUYS band can be a retailer!! Now we are familiar with the retailers that are an easy competitor for BIG GUYS! What are the BGs(Big Guys)’s plan for them? Simple again! They interest everyone into trading and teach them how to do it wrong! Brokers are some of their best friends! Brokers make the deal and profit from the spreads and commissions! Easy money for them ! So if they have enough customers they can make more money. Ok. Simple work! Just try to advertise for trading and show it as simple as possible! They try to provide sexy platforms and apps for trading and lots of artistic indicator! You can interest everybody into trading just by a simple indicator and a beautiful trend line or channel! They translate their false trading strategies that are prepared very smart into many language and give them to you just for free! Yeah they really love retailers in the deepest part of their hearts! They show you: oh look at this beautiful channel. Every time price hit one side, it will react there and will go to the other side. Also look at that beautiful MACD, RSI, … indicator that is telling you when to trade and when to exit! So amazing. Who is enough silly not to start trading after seeing this! Absolutely no one as most of us did! : ) So let’s start with the free education and after a short period of time we can start trading and open a live account to make the millions that we were dreaming all the times! Ok a good and sexy plan! But most of the retailers don’t know who are behind the sense that are playing the games! Smart and experienced traders teams with millions in their asset on their trading desk! They are there for retail trader’s money and will make them confused each time! Retailers: Damn! my stop just got hit by a few pips and then price went in my direction! Ok. That because the lake of my understanding. I will study more and will do it again. There is a huge amount of money right there, I can’t give up on it simply! That is the story that will be repeat again and again and HAS TO be repeat to full the pocket of BGs and their friends! Just simple as always! ........................................................................................................................................................................................... So as an advantage of knowing the main players in the market and their goals: We as Smart retailers : ) have to be aware of all the tricks that are played in the market As RTM told us many times, we have to try to get into the mind of the BGs and put their hats on our heads and look at the chart as ones does! We have to think in terms of order flow. Where BGs can fill their orders? Where they can trap others to provide opposite positions for them to fill their orders? RTM showed us how to see the manipulation of hope and fear and think about the retailer’s mindset. How to see the buildings of positions and the real movement of price and the reasons behind them If we study all the RTM concepts they are all saying that what are BGs doing in the market and where they are looking for trapping other retailers RTM is telling us always looking for signs of what the BGs are doing at the significant areas and emphasize on it that if we don’t see what they are doing, there will be no trade for us! If you seek through RTM and all the Concepts and the trades IF and masters took you can see it clearly what is going on the market and you will understand how to READ THE MARKET properly This was just a brief and simple explanation of what I have learned so far from this community and want to share it with my friends You can read more through RTM and some very limited sources in the Internet Know your enemies and their intentions and don't let them fool you : ) Best Regards omid Attachments: Check this box to be notified of replies to this topic. Hi, Here is what I see: ORFL -Down to the left on a lower time frame: We have a reaction (supply) and a reaction to the supply (Origin). Origin is tested and bounces price (ORFL). Then, ORFL Down is itself engulfed and a new FL Box (red) is formed. When the red FL Box it engulfed another FL Box is formed and we have a swap zone. Like before, we have a reaction to a FL (the FL is neon green). There is also an engulf of a very small ORFL down, which occurs before the larger reaction to the green FL. Just like before, price engulfs this zone and another ORFL is formed. Test 1 to create supply: After supply is formed by testing the ORFL Down, an opportunity to FO to MPL (deeper in to the ORFL) exists and price reacts accordingly. I think this is why Omid went short at that level. Attachments: Check this box to be notified of replies to this topic. Note: BBcode and smileys are still usable. The following user(s) said Thank You: pardis , cuimingzhao "Cobalah terus sampai kegagalanmu habis tak bersisa" mangdsd Report to moderator Quick Reply Reply Quote Thank You Fight Club 01 Jan 2017 16:04 #224 hoeschec --> Offline Associate Trader Posts: 1795 Thank you received: 2480 samjardavid wrote: hi hoeschec ^_^ what the different between ORFL and FL? is ORFL more important than FL Thank you in advance David Hi Samjardavid- A FL is any FTR that holds price up or down whereas a ORFL is an origin up/down and a FL. ORFL is often the target of longs/shorts, so they hold special importance. Salivan discusses the concept of magnetic attraction vs. repulsion and this is related specifically to ORFL's.
